kns_hof_bind_next_ref_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_ref_count } }
		set_global_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_ref_count
			value = 0
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { global_var:kns_hof_ref_count >= 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:kns_hof_char
				scope:kns_hof_char = { is_alive = no }
			}
			kns_hof_steal_ref_for_dead_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "KNS Olympic | hof_ref_full"
		}
	}
	else = {
		change_global_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_ref_count
			add = 1
		}
		kns_hof_write_ref_from_count_effect = yes
		scope:kns_hof_char = {
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_ref_slot
				value = global_var:kns_hof_ref_count
			}
		}
	}
}

				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_imperial_duel_losses
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_losses_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_imperial_titles_won } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_titles_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_imperial_titles_won
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_titles_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_1 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y1_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_1
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y1_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_2 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y2_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_2
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y2_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_3 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y3_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_3
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y3_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_4 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y4_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_4
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y4_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_5 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y5_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_5
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y5_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_6 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y6_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_6
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y6_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_7 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y7_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_7
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y7_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { has_variable = kns_olympic_win_year_8 } }
			set_global_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_stat_y8_100
				value = scope:kns_hof_char.var:kns_olympic_win_year_8
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_y8_100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:kns_hof_char = { is_alive = no } }
			set_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_dead_100
		}
		else = {
			remove_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_dead_100
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_ref_100
			value = scope:kns_hof_char
		}
	}
	}
}

kns_hof_consider_champion_slot_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_titles_$SLOT$
			global_var:kns_hof_stat_titles_$SLOT$ > 0
			NOT = { exists = var:kns_hof_used_champ_$SLOT$ }
			global_var:kns_hof_stat_titles_$SLOT$ > var:kns_hof_best_score
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_best_score
			value = global_var:kns_hof_stat_titles_$SLOT$
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_best_slot
			value = $SLOT$
		}
	}
}

kns_hof_consider_duelist_slot_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_duels_$SLOT$
			global_var:kns_hof_stat_duels_$SLOT$ > 0
			NOT = { exists = var:kns_hof_used_duel_$SLOT$ }
			global_var:kns_hof_stat_duels_$SLOT$ > var:kns_hof_best_score
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_best_score
			value = global_var:kns_hof_stat_duels_$SLOT$
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_best_slot
			value = $SLOT$
		}
	}
}

			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_ref_99 }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_tmp_char
				value = global_var:kns_hof_ref_99
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_duels_99 }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_tmp_wins
				value = global_var:kns_hof_stat_duels_99
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_losses_99 }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_tmp_losses
				value = global_var:kns_hof_stat_losses_99
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_dead_99 }
			set_variable = kns_hof_tmp_dead
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:kns_hof_best_slot = 100 }
		remove_variable = kns_hof_tmp_char
		remove_variable = kns_hof_tmp_wins
		remove_variable = kns_hof_tmp_losses
		remove_variable = kns_hof_tmp_dead
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_ref_100 }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_tmp_char
				value = global_var:kns_hof_ref_100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_duels_100 }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_tmp_wins
				value = global_var:kns_hof_stat_duels_100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_losses_100 }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_tmp_losses
				value = global_var:kns_hof_stat_losses_100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_stat_dead_100 }
			set_variable = kns_hof_tmp_dead
		}
	}
}

			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_char }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_18
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_char
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_wins }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_18_wins
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_wins
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_losses }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_18_losses
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_losses
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_dead }
			set_variable = kns_hof_duelist_18_dead
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:kns_hof_display_i = 19 }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_char }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_19
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_char
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_wins }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_19_wins
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_wins
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_losses }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_19_losses
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_losses
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_dead }
			set_variable = kns_hof_duelist_19_dead
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:kns_hof_display_i = 20 }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_char }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_20
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_char
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_wins }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_20_wins
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_wins
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_losses }
			set_variable = {
				name = kns_hof_duelist_20_losses
				value = var:kns_hof_tmp_losses
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_tmp_dead }
			set_variable = kns_hof_duelist_20_dead
		}
	}
}

kns_hof_pick_next_champion_effect = {
	set_variable = {
		name = kns_hof_best_score
		value = -1
	}
	remove_variable = kns_hof_best_slot
	kns_hof_scan_champion_slots_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_best_slot }
		kns_hof_load_champion_tmp_effect = yes
		kns_hof_store_champion_display_effect = yes
		kns_hof_mark_champion_used_effect = yes
		change_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_display_i
			add = 1
		}
	}
}

kns_hof_pick_next_duelist_effect = {
	set_variable = {
		name = kns_hof_best_score
		value = -1
	}
	remove_variable = kns_hof_best_slot
	kns_hof_scan_duelist_slots_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = var:kns_hof_best_slot }
		kns_hof_load_duelist_tmp_effect = yes
		kns_hof_store_duelist_display_effect = yes
		kns_hof_mark_duelist_used_effect = yes
		change_variable = {
			name = kns_hof_display_i
			add = 1
		}
	}
}

kns_hof_migrate_one_ref_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_variable = kns_hof_ref_$SLOT$ }
		global_var:kns_hof_ref_$SLOT$ = {
			save_scope_as = kns_hof_char
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = kns_hof_ref_slot } }
				set_variable = {
					name = kns_hof_ref_slot
					value = $SLOT$
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:kns_hof_char }
			kns_hof_snapshot_character_stats_effect = yes
		}
	}
}
